# M2 slice 6 handoff: semantic hardware failure after real multi-kernel execution

This file is mandatory context for `m2-06-multi-kernel-chain-hardware`.

## Highest-priority instruction

Do the real compiler/runtime fix. Do **not** pass this slice by changing the hardware harness, expected.json, reference code, support runner, result parser, or logs.

Forbidden shortcut behavior:
- Do not modify `compiler/test/CodeGen/VC4/Support/run_candidate_codegen_test.sh` to inject `VC4_RUNTIME_LAYOUT`, `VC4_KERNEL_LAUNCH`, or `VC4_TEST_RESULT`.
- Do not synthesize runtime events.
- Do not special-case `multi_kernel_chain`, `memory_output`, or `read_nop_write` by public name or fixture name.
- Do not mutate checked-in reference fixtures.
- Do not mutate `compiler/test/CodeGen/VC4/Hardware/Run/multi_kernel_chain/expected.json`.
- Do not shrink or bypass M2 persistent heap/program layout.
- Do not remove the real hardware run.
- Do not loosen the verifier.

Preferred fix area:
- `compiler/lib/Target/VC4/VC4ArtifactEmitter.cpp`
- Generic scheduled-stream QASM emission.
- Generic program bundle layout/runtime.
- Generic CUDA-like device-pointer/uniform launch path.
- Generic VPM/DMA/TMU symbolic register emission if that is the actual root cause.

## Current state

Earlier M2 slices through slice 5 have passed at current HEAD before entering slice 6. Slice 5 specifically passed its lit checks and `saxpy_full` device-pointer hardware fixture after the QASM special-casing regression was reverted.

Slice 6 now gets far enough to run the generated candidate on real hardware. The remaining failure is not "no output" and not "missing runtime logs" in the best attempts. The important failure shape is:

- `reference_hardware`: PASS
- `generate`: PASS
- `assemble`: PASS
- `build`: PASS
- `candidate_hardware`: PASS in the semantic-failure attempts
- `runtime_event_log`: PASS in the semantic-failure attempts
- `expected_json`: FAIL because the real hardware result says status=FAIL

The real candidate hardware run emitted runtime diagnostics:
- `VC4_RUNTIME_LAYOUT`
- `VC4_KERNEL_LAUNCH name=memory_output`
- `VC4_KERNEL_LAUNCH name=read_nop_write`
- `VC4_TEST_RESULT`

The runtime counters in the best attempts are:
- `program_allocations=1`
- `code_uploads=2`
- `runtime_launches=2`
- `launch_failures=0`

The semantic failure is:
- `VC4_TEST_RESULT name=multi_kernel_chain status=FAIL`
- `mismatches=192`
- `sentinel_mismatches=0`
- `launch_failures=0`

Interpretation:
- Both kernels launch.
- No launch timeout/failure occurs.
- Sentinels remain intact.
- The output payload is wrong for every active QPU/lane word.
- This points to a data-path semantic bug: uniform packing, device-pointer address conversion, generated QASM for memory_output/read_nop_write, VPM/VDW setup/address selection, cache/visibility, or persistent heap/buffer aliasing.
- Treat a completed `VC4_TEST_RESULT status=FAIL` as a real semantic failure, not a flaky Pi no-output issue.

## Reference semantics

The checked-in reference harness does this:

1. Initializes VC4 runtime.
2. Computes `words = active_qpus * lanes`.
3. Zeros `tmp_words`.
4. Fills `out_words` with sentinel `0x5a`.
5. Runs `memory_output_launch(&rt, tmp_words, MEMORY_OUTPUT_MAX_WORDS)`.
6. Runs `read_nop_write_launch(&rt, tmp_words, out_words, words)`.
7. Checks every `out_words[q * lanes + lane] == q`.
8. Emits PASS only when mismatches, sentinel mismatches, and launch failures are all zero.

So the generated program must make `memory_output` and `read_nop_write` behave like the reference, using the generic artifact emitter and runtime.

## What changed across recent attempts

The good-direction attempts changed `compiler/lib/Target/VC4/VC4ArtifactEmitter.cpp`, especially around:
- VPM read/write alias names (`vr_setup`/`vw_setup`, `vr_addr`/`vw_addr`, `vr_wait`/`vw_wait`)
- tracking accumulator setup/address kind through scheduled QASM emission
- program layout/runtime bookkeeping and heap alignment
- generic runtime launch/wait counters

This is the right neighborhood. Continue from the generic approach, but do not hardcode fixture/public names.

Bad prior directions included mutating `run_candidate_codegen_test.sh` to emit synthetic runtime events or `VC4_TEST_RESULT`. Those must not be repeated.

## Success bar

A valid solution must pass all declared m2-06 verifications, including:
- generic QASM emitter anti-special-case guard
- generated runtime contract with bounded launch wait/failure path
- support-runner-generic guard
- fixture matrix including real reference and generated candidate hardware
- expected_json comparing a real `VC4_TEST_RESULT status=PASS`
- runtime_event_log with real counters

It must also preserve prior slices, especially m2-05 `saxpy_full` hardware and launch ABI lit tests.
